# Gold set: what is each list's side on the statement?

For each pair: the statement, the list, and the record of the list's candidates who were MKs in Knessets 24–25 on the topic: bills they sponsored and their votes on private bills. Decide from substance whether the list, by this record, agrees or disagrees with the statement, or the record does not show a side (unclear). Bills not about the statement's question should be ignored.

## G1: list Zionut Datit, headed by Bezelel Smotrich, and Zehut, headed by Moshe Feiglin — Local tax discounts
Statement: Reservists, people with disabilities and other residents should get bigger discounts on municipal property tax and parking, even if local councils have less money.
The list has 6 former MKs among its candidates.

## G3: list Likud , headed by Binyamin Netanyahu for Prime Minister — Disability and sickness benefits
Statement: More people should qualify for disability, nursing-care and sick-leave benefits, for example homemakers and the self-employed, even if National Insurance spends more.
The list has 34 former MKs among its candidates.

## G4: list Ra’am, the United Arab List — Protection of debtors
Statement: Debtors should be better protected — for example no electricity cut-offs for unpaid bills and no travel bans over small debts — even if creditors find it harder to collect.
The list has 6 former MKs among its candidates.

## G5: list Ra’am, the United Arab List — Disability and sickness benefits
Statement: More people should qualify for disability, nursing-care and sick-leave benefits, for example homemakers and the self-employed, even if National Insurance spends more.
The list has 6 former MKs among its candidates.
Bills sponsored by them on this topic (9; up to 25 shown):

## G19: list HaDemocratim, headed by Yair Golan — Protection of debtors
Statement: Debtors should be better protected — for example no electricity cut-offs for unpaid bills and no travel bans over small debts — even if creditors find it harder to collect.
The list has 11 former MKs among its candidates.

## G22: list Yahadut HaTorah v’ha’Shabbat (United Torah Judaism) – Agudat Yisrael and Degel HaTorah — Overriding court rulings
Statement: The Knesset should be able to re-enact a law struck down by the Supreme Court, even if citizens have fewer ways to challenge laws in court.
The list has 9 former MKs among its candidates.

## G23: list HaDemocratim, headed by Yair Golan — Rights of suspects
Statement: Suspects and defendants should have fewer legal protections during arrest, questioning and trial, even if more innocent people are wrongly accused.
The list has 11 former MKs among its candidates.

## G31: list Likud , headed by Binyamin Netanyahu for Prime Minister — Funding schools without the core curriculum
Statement: Schools that do not teach the core curriculum (math, English) should not receive state funding, even if students' families have to pay more for tuition.
